11: public class GTUtilityTest {
13: @Test
14: void testPowIntPositive() {
15:   assertEquals(GTUtility.powInt(0.95, 1), Math.pow(0.95, 1));
16:   assertEquals(GTUtility.powInt(0.95, 2), Math.pow(0.95, 2));
17:   assertEquals(GTUtility.powInt(0.95, 3), Math.pow(0.95, 3));

45: @Test
46: public void testBeamFade() {
47:   assertEquals(0.0, VoidcraftShipFx.beamFade(0.0), 1e-12, "off at leg start");
48:   assertEquals(0.0, VoidcraftShipFx.beamFade(1.0), 1e-12, "off at leg end");
49:   assertEquals(1.0, VoidcraftShipFx.beamFade(0.5), 1e-12, "fully on mid-leg");
53:   assertTrue(VoidcraftShipFx.beamFade(ramp / 2.0) < 1.0, "still ramping in the first ramp region");
54:   assertTrue(VoidcraftShipFx.beamFade(1.0 - ramp / 2.0) < 1.0, "still ramping in the last ramp region");
56:   assertEquals(1.0, VoidcraftShipFx.beamFade(ramp), 1e-12, "fully on at the end of the ramp");
57:   assertEquals(1.0, VoidcraftShipFx.beamFade(1.0 - ramp), 1e-12, "fully on at the start of the release ramp");
63:   assertTrue(v >= prev - 1e-12, "rising ramp monotone at p=" + p);
68:   assertEquals(VoidcraftShipFx.beamFade(p), VoidcraftShipFx.beamFade(1.0 - p), 1e-12, "symmetric at p=" + p);
70: END
72: @Test
73: public void testBeamBasisIsOrthonormal() {
80:   assertNotNull(b, "non-degenerate endpoints produce a basis: " + c[0] + "," + c[1] + "," + c[2]);
90:   assertEquals(1.0, norm(dx, dy, dz), 1e-9, "direction is unit length");
91:   assertEquals(1.0, norm(p1x, p1y, p1z), 1e-9, "p1 is unit length");
92:   assertEquals(1.0, norm(p2x, p2y, p2z), 1e-9, "p2 is unit length");
93:   assertEquals(0.0, dot(dx, dy, dz, p1x, p1y, p1z), 1e-9, "p1 perpendicular to direction");
94:   assertEquals(0.0, dot(dx, dy, dz, p2x, p2y, p2z), 1e-9, "p2 perpendicular to direction");
95:   assertEquals(0.0, dot(p1x, p1y, p1z, p2x, p2y, p2z), 1e-9, "p1 perpendicular to p2");
100:   assertEquals(dx, cx, 1e-9, "right-handed x");
101:   assertEquals(dy, cy, 1e-9, "right-handed y");
102:   assertEquals(dz, cz, 1e-9, "right-handed z");
104:   assertNull(
105:   VoidcraftShipFx.beamBasis(new double[] { 1.0, 2.0, 3.0 }, new double[] { 1.0, 2.0, 3.0 }),
106:   "coincident endpoints are degenerate -> null");
107: END

230: END

78:   assertTrue(cond(USSConditionOp.LT, "3", "10").evaluate(" 2 ", "3"), "sides are trimmed before parsing");
79:   assertTrue(cond(USSConditionOp.LT, "3", "10").evaluate("-5", "1"), "negatives compare numerically");
80:   assertFalse(cond(USSConditionOp.LT, "3", "10").evaluate("10", "3"));
81:   assertFalse(cond(USSConditionOp.LT, "3", "10").evaluate("5", "5"), "equal values are neither LT");
82: END

131:   assertEquals(99.25, restored.z(), 1e-12);
132: END

258:   "unwritten slots read as the empty string");
259:   assertEquals(
260:   0,
261:   uss.getVariables()
262:   .writtenCount());
263:   assertFalse(
264:   uss.getVariables()
265:   .isWritten(0));
266: END
268: @Test
269: public void testWithVariablesIsImmutableAndPreservedByWithChain() {
275:   assertNotSame(uss, v, "withVariables is copy-on-write");
276:   assertEquals(
277:   "hello",
278:   v.getVariables()
279:   .get(0));
280:   assertEquals(
281:   "edge",
282:   v.getVariables()
283:   .get(255),
284:   "slot 255 is in range");
285:   assertEquals(
286:   2,
287:   v.getVariables()
288:   .writtenCount());
289:   assertEquals(
290:   0,
291:   uss.getVariables()
292:   .writtenCount(),
293:   "the original is unaffected");
296:   assertEquals(
297:   "hello",
298:   more.getVariables()
299:   .get(0),
300:   "the with* chain preserves the variable space");
301: END
303: @Test
304: public void testVariableSpaceNbtRoundTripIsSparse() {
313:   assertEquals(2, list.tagCount(), "SPARSE â€” only written slots are serialized (not 256)");
315:   assertNotNull(loaded);
316:   assertEquals(
317:   "a",
318:   loaded.getVariables()
319:   .get(10));
320:   assertEquals(
321:   "b",
322:   loaded.getVariables()
323:   .get(200));
324:   assertEquals(
325:   "",
326:   loaded.getVariables()
327:   .get(11),
328:   "an unwritten slot stays empty");
329:   assertEquals(
330:   2,
331:   loaded.getVariables()
332:   .writtenCount());
333: END
